# Yang–Mills existence: independent attempt (started 2026-10-27)

Trigger: OpenAI post of 2026-10-26 claims an internal model proved existence of 4D pure YM (NL proof Sept 9, Lean Oct 16), QCD (Sept 18), and an SM-completing GUT, building on Balaban. Artifacts not public (Gross/Douglas/Jaffe/Klebanov/Nekrasov group holds them). Elvin's ask: attempt it, focus on YM.

## Status
- 2026-10-27: lit gate running (Douglas 2026 update, Clay statement, Balaban/Dimock/MRS statements, Euclidean-invariance techniques, fermions). Scratch in /tmp/oai_oct26/. Nothing derived yet.

## Working decomposition (to be revised after lit gate)
A. Target statement (finite volume first): Wilson-loop Schwinger functions of 4D SU(N) lattice YM on torus of side L, spacing ε, Wilson action, coupling g(ε) from asymptotic freedom; limit ε→0 exists along a subsequence, is nontrivial, reflection positive, and Euclidean invariant in the interior.
B. Balaban UV stability = bounds on effective actions/partition function. Must be extended to Wilson-loop insertions (check whether Balaban 1988 "generating functional" covers this).
C. Subsequential existence is cheap (|W| ≤ N); content = nontriviality + regularity + invariance.
D. Nontriviality for small loops = rigorous asymptotic freedom at scale r: 1 − ⟨W_r⟩ ≍ g(r)^2 r^0 (to check).
E. Euclidean invariance: hardest for lattice constructions; options = convergence with rates + orientation independence, or Ward identities, or continuum-invariant regularization (MRS 1993).
F. Infinite volume + OS clustering: separate; may not be needed for "existence".

## Rules
- Every claim marked [lit] with citation, [derived] with a verifier pass, or [guess].
- No headline until a Fable verifier signs off.

## Revised decomposition
Route = compactness (admissible per fn. 2) + (i) nontriviality + (ii) regularity + (iii) Euclidean invariance + (iv) OS reconstruction, all in finite volume T4 first.

## Euclidean invariance + Balaban scope (lit, 2026-10-27, agent Opus unless [V])
- Lattice limits give only hypercubic symmetry + reflection positivity. Three known mechanisms for full O(4): (a) identify with a rotation-invariant continuum regularization; (b) orientation-independence of the limit, which needs UNIQUENESS of the limit, not compactness (BFS-II 1980 suggested "lattices of varying orientation"); (c) uniqueness of invariant measure of a rotation-covariant dynamic (Duch–Hairer–Yi–Zhao arXiv:2508.07776 Thm 2.18 for Φ⁴₃, small coupling). Route (c) is unavailable in 4D YM: the Langevin dynamic is critical, regularity structures stop at d=3 (CCHS 2022). Route (a) fails: MRS 1993 is axial gauge, "not Euclidean invariant", no OS, IR cutoff never lifted, gauge-fixed correlators only, Slavnov identities = main result. → Route (b) or a new idea is forced. Jaffe–Witten fn. 2 forbids compactness alone, but uniqueness of the limit would satisfy fn. 2 and route (b) at once.
- Balaban 4D (CMP 109, 116, 119, 122): effective-action densities incl. large fields on a torus, bounds uniform in spacing; no observables (MRS: his UV limit "not necessarily unique"). Jaffe–Witten [V]: "need to be extended to the study of expectations of gauge-invariant functions".
- Fermions: Dimock QED₃ = U(1) Wilson fermions, partition-function bounds only. No non-abelian + Wilson fermion work in Balaban's framework. Chiral SU(5): no rigorous route.
- Nontriviality: no lattice gauge construction in d≥3 has a nontrivial limit (Chatterjee [V]); Φ⁴₃ non-Gaussianity proofs use superrenormalizability.

Wait for O3_TEMPLATE.md: how the Oct 4 O(3) paper (also asymptotically free, also critical for SPDE) obtains uniqueness + rotation invariance. That mechanism is the most likely YM mechanism.

## Reverse-engineered mechanism from the O(3) template ([V] read §1.4, §10.2, §10.3 verbatim, 2026-10-27)
Euclidean invariance = route (b), made cheap by two tricks:
1. UNIQUENESS: the RG trajectory converges (exact blocking with sources, canonical normalization, "almost dimension-two contraction" per step, Lemma 12.2 summable sensitivity of the marginal coupling). Limit laws are determined by moments (factorial moment bounds ⇒ analytic MGF). No compactness alone.
2. ROTATION: two "inclined first blockings" with Pythagorean rotation O+ (cos 3/5, sin 4/5) and its reflection O− produce a common coarse frame; after the first step ordinary blockings erase the difference ⇒ the plane limits in orientations 1 and O+² agree ⇒ invariance under rotation by 2θ; 2cosθ = 6/5 not an integer ⇒ θ/π irrational ⇒ dense subgroup ⇒ continuity on Schwartz space (bound 10.9) gives full O(2). Reflections + quarter turns from the lattice.
3. OS: reflection positivity from lattice (chessboard, FILS); clustering from a lattice gap uniform in cutoff (§8); reconstruction per Osterwalder–Schrader with linear growth condition.
4. Nontriviality: separated fourth cumulant survives the limit (§11.3) via sources carried through the exact RG (§9).
Transfer to YM4: steps 1–2 are the generic core. Step 3 clustering needs a gap → NOT available for YM → finite-volume (torus) OS instead. Step 4 analogue: connected 4-point function of a gauge-invariant local density (tr F²) or Wilson loops.
Candidate attack: "inclined first blocking" for lattice gauge fields = Balaban averaging on a Pythagorean-rotated sublattice (index 25 in a coordinate plane), plus the abstract dense-rotation lemma.

## O(3) template read (agent Fable, reports/O3_TEMPLATE.md, 2026-10-27)
- Theorem shape: Thm 1.2 = construction along a shooting trajectory with terminal block coupling H fixed at physical length 1 (Λ-parameter scheme); Thm 1.1 = canonical normalization + uniqueness over all β via an incommensurability lemma (ordinary step shifts offset by log L/2π, inclined step by d with length factor 5; 5^{-q}=L^p impossible ⇒ offset-independence).
- Fixed-torus continuum limit (Prop 10.2 first part) needs NO gap and NO reflection positivity: only fixed-torus moment bounds + exact sources + Cauchy formula.
- Everything on R² (E4, Hamiltonian gap, canonical normalization) rests on a rate-controlled lattice mass gap (Prop 2.1, X(β) ≤ C e^{(4π−η)β}) + RP transfer doubling. No analogue for YM without a gap.
- Nontriviality: four terminal cells, block spins uniformly distributed on S² at terminal scale ⇒ fourth cumulant −2/15 + o(1). YM analogue: terminal coupling O(1) at physical length 1 ⇒ non-Gaussian plaquette/Wilson-loop statistics at that scale.
- Predicted YM claim shape: Theorem-1.2-type on a fixed T⁴ (or spatial torus × R): Balaban block RG with retained coupling, shooting to terminal coupling at length 1, endpoint matching, exact sources for smeared tr F²/Wilson loops, fixed-torus convergence, E2/E3 from Wilson RP, unique vacuum from transfer positivity on a cylinder. R⁴ + E4 would need an infinite-volume mechanism absent here (consistent with Woit's "existence for small volume" guess).
- Generic (transferable) pieces: exact block RG with polymer gas; shooting + endpoint matching; bounded-offset retuning; exact source transport; fixed-torus convergence via sources; Pythagorean inclined-blocking rotation argument; RP transfer doubling; OS reconstruction; incommensurability uniqueness lemma.
- Non-transferable: Mermin–Wagner/log-energy mixing estimate, Griffiths positivity, S²-uniform nontriviality, O(2) tangent inversion for the L^{-2} contraction, 2D transfer identities.

- SOURCES_A15_LOC.md (Opus, unverified, text layer only): (Loc) PROVED EXACT (not up to exponential) relative to six structural inputs. CMP 119 p. 278 is a per-term statement with no error term (terms that fail it go whole into B^{(k+1)}); the "exponentially small irrelevant" booking is at the coefficient level only ((4.36), p. 281). Mechanism: the s-rule of CMP 116 p. 3 — every walk term carries s(Δ) of each cube its domain meets; at s = 0 outside Y_0 every propagator is a walk sum inside Y_0; the nonlinear maps D, A_0, H_k(B′) solve local equations splitting by components; the Mayer/polymer assembly (2.1)–(2.13) uses only cube geometry inside X; torus image terms come only from winding walks, which feed only wrapping activities. Theorem LOC: E^{(k+1)} on T_N at X = E^{(k+1)} on Z^4 at the lift X̂ exactly when X + a one-cube collar fits in a box of side < N/2; induction on k via four lemmas (walk sums, nonlinear maps, Gaussian integrals/determinants, assembly/subtractions); Lemma S: the step stays exact because the torus recursion subtracts the Z^d number β. Consequence: Prop. A15-N no longer needs (Loc) as an assumption; the Z^d limit at g > 0 (row A5's assertion) FOLLOWS. Inputs: (FI) each walk factor from the same local formula on T_N and Z^4 (CMP 99 Sect. C not re-read); (U) uniqueness of the [15] fixed points; (Det) the [16] determinant expansion follows the s-rule ([16] unread); (Cube) [14] cube maps use only □_0 data; (Cases) "in exactly the same way"; (Conv) cube sides divide N (M_1 unchecked). - SOURCES_T2_GAPRIME2.md (Opus, unverified, STATUS: COMPLETE): (G_A′) PROVED for small |f| with explicit constants. For any c < 1 (units |ef| = 2|f|), A′ = H′ + D′D′^† has no spectrum in (−c|ef|, c|ef|) for every real f with 0 < |ef| ≤ ((1 − c)/100)²; by invariance of ker D′^† the gap holds for H′ on ker D′^† (e.g. gap ≥ |f| for |f| ≤ 1.25e−5); independent of j, L, averaging; no rational flux, no Harper/Helffer–Sjöstrand input. Steps: explicit operator A′ = cos φ·c^†c + D′D′^† − 2 sin φ·K (Wilson action, Landau gauge on Z²); scalar Landau gap via a lattice ladder operator Π = ∇_0 + i sgn(α)∇_1 with X(X − 2|α|) ≥ −157|α|³ on low states (second-/third-order magnetic regularity lemmas) → no spectrum in [(1 + θ)|α|, (3 − θ)|α|] for θ > 26|α|^{1/2} + 157|α|; reduction A′ = 𝔏 ⊗ 1 − 2ασ_y + R with ‖Rψ‖ ≤ 48|α|^{3/2} near 0; contradiction argument. Numerics (Mac, < 1 min): gap2d JSON reproduced exactly; formula matches the finite-difference Hessian to 1e−7; H′D′ = 0 to 3e−14; Harper Bloch blocks at 11 fluxes p/q, q ≤ 2213: smallest |eigenvalue| = |α|(1 − 0.125|α|) at every flux; band 2 from |α|(3 − 0.62|α|); measured ‖Rψ‖ ≈ 2.3|α|^{3/2} (proved constants crude but right in order). The Nielsen–Olesen mode is the physical band at −|ef|(1 + 0.12|ef|), not a zero mode; gauge directions excluded by ker D′^†. Open [N]: the 3|ef| upper edge, sharp constants; inherited [I]: S123's midpoint-transport A′ = script operator up to gauge. EFFECT: S123 O1 CLOSED → Prop. 5.6 and (Z_j) proved given S123's inputs; (M_j) reduces to (I3) [P] + BETA_FP_M Prop. 4.4 region 1 [argued] + the neutral-sector [I]. Row B4 to update after verification. → verifier B76 (priority: the ladder-operator inequality X(X − 2|α|) ≥ −157|α|³ and the regularity lemmas; the remainder bound 48|α|^{3/2}; the contradiction argument; rerun the script).

Region 1 = a momentum/spectrum range (|K|² ≥ R′|f| / ≥ Rμ), an asymptotic identity: log|x| = F_s + g̃_μ + ψ with s = |f|^{3/4}, F_s = Σc_k log(x + ks); g̃_μ = O(|f|³ log) by S123 5.5/5.6 + (G_A′); ψ = O(s³ + |f|³) (Lemma 2.2, (S3) + 4D IDS, needs a > 2/3); F_s gapped at σ ≥ |f|^{3/4}, expanded by an approximate Moyal inverse (per-cell trace of two twisted kernels has no phase → per-momentum integral); evenness in f + dominated convergence σ → 0 give the f² coefficient −2n_2d_j with error O(|f|^{9/4}) + o(1)f²; Prop. 4.4 holds as n_2d_jf² + o(f²) at fixed j (its printed rate O(|f|³ log) not proved, unused); S123 Lemma 7.2 no longer needed. Neutral sector: (N2) every f-derivative of the neutral symbol ≤ C_p|K̂|² by induction over the one-step recursion (bordered-inverse derivative); differentiation under the integral justified, error O(f⁴); finite-torus zero modes = d constant 1-forms (f-independent, not gauge, not lifted); on the infinite lattice K = 0 is null, N^{neu}(λ) ≤ Cλ², no atom. Numerics t2_mjrest_neutral.py (1.6 s): spectrum = ∪_K{l, cos φ·l} to 5e−8, exactly 2 zero modes, gap cos φ·2(1 − cos(2π/N)). → verifier B85 (priority: Lemma A Step 2 ‖R_0W·v^{[2]}‖ ≤ C via ER; Step 3 Moyal-order cancellation bookkeeping; Lemma 2.2's Stieltjes step; the (N2) bordered-inverse chain).
